<div className="bible-chapter" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-lang="en" data-translation="nmv" data-translation-id="eng_nmv" data-translation-label="NMV" data-book="2CH" data-book-slug="2-chronicles" data-book-name="2 Chronicles" data-chapter="1" data-verses="822">

<div className="bt">
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; the son of David &#91;beloved&#93; was strengthened in his kingdom, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;was&#93; with him, and magnified him exceedingly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> Then Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; spoke unto all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, to the captains of thousands and of hundreds, and to the judges, and to every governor in all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, the chief of the fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> So Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, and all the congregation with him, went to the high place that &#91;was&#93; at Giv’on &#91;Hill City&#93;; for there was the tabernacle of the congregation of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, which Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; the servant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had made in the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> But the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; had David &#91;beloved&#93; brought up from Kiryat-Ye’arim &#91;City of Forests&#93; to &#91;the place which&#93; David &#91;beloved&#93; had prepared for it: for he had pitched a tent for it at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> Moreover the brasen altar, that B’tzal’el &#91;In The Shadow (Protection) of El / God&#93; the son of Uri &#91;my light, my fire&#93;, the son of Hur &#91;Liberty&#93;, had made, he put before the tabernacle of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; and the congregation sought unto it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; went up there to the brasen altar before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which &#91;was&#93; at the tabernacle of the congregation, and offered a thousand burnt offerings upon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> In that night did God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; appear unto Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, and said unto him, Ask what I shall give youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; said unto God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, youi have showed great mercy unto David &#91;beloved&#93; my father, and have made me to reign in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> Now, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, let youi promise unto David &#91;beloved&#93; my father be established: for youi have made me king over a people like the dust of the earth in multitude.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> Give me now wisdom and knowledge, that I may go out and come in before this people: for who can judge this youri people, &#91;that is so&#93; great?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> And God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; said to Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, Because this was in youi heart, and youi have not asked riches, wealth, or honour, nor the life of youi enemies, neither yet have asked long life; but have asked wisdom and knowledge for yourself, that youi may judge my people, over whom I have made youi king:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> Wisdom and knowledge &#91;is&#93; granted unto youi; and I will give youi riches, and wealth, and honour, such as none of the kings have had that &#91;have been&#93; before youi, neither shall there any after youi have the like.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> Then Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; came &#91;from his journey&#93; to the high place that &#91;was&#93; at Giv’on &#91;Hill City&#93; to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, from before the tabernacle of the congregation, and reigned over Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; gathered chariots and horsemen: and he had a thousand and four hundred chariots, and twelve thousand horsemen, which he placed in the chariot cities, and with the king at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And the king made silver and gold at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; &#91;as plenteous&#93; as stones, and cedar trees made he as the sycomore trees that &#91;are&#93; in the vale for abundance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; had horses brought out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and linen yarn: the king’s merchants received the linen yarn at a price.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> And they fetched up, and brought forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; a chariot for six hundred &#91;shekels&#93; of silver, and an horse for an hundred and fifty: and so brought they out &#91;horses&#93; for all the kings of the Hitti &#91;Terrorists&#93;, and for the kings of Aram &#91;exalted&#93; (Syria) &#91;exalted&#93;, by their means.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; determined to build an house for the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and an house for his kingdom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; tallied threescore and ten thousand men to bear burdens, and eighty thousand to hew in the mountain, and three thousand and six hundred to oversee them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; sent to Huram &#91;noble born&#93; the king of Tzor &#91;a rock&#93;, saying, As youi did deal with David &#91;beloved&#93; my father, and did send him cedars to build him an house to dwell in it, &#91;even so deal with me&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> Behold, I build an house to the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, to dedicate &#91;it&#93; to him, &#91;and&#93; to burn before him sweet incense, and for the continual showbread, and for the burnt offerings morning and evening, on the Shabbat, and on the new moons, and on the solemn feasts of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;. This &#91;is an ordinance&#93; forever to Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And the house which I build &#91;is&#93; great: for great &#91;is&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; above all gods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> But who is able to build him an house, seeing the heaven and heaven of heavens cannot contain him? who &#91;am&#93; I then, that I should build him an house, save only to burn sacrifice before him?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> Send me now therefore a man cunning to work in gold, and in silver, and in brass, and in iron, and in purple, and crimson, and blue, and that can skill to grave with the cunning men that &#91;are&#93; with me in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, whom David &#91;beloved&#93; my father did provide.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> Send me also cedar trees, fir trees, and algum trees, out of L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;: for I know that youi servants can skill to cut timber in L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;; and, behold, my servants &#91;shall be&#93; with youi servants,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> Even to prepare me timber in abundance: for the house which I am about to build &#91;shall be&#93; wonderful great.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> And, behold, I will give to youi servants, the hewers that cut timber, twenty thousand measures of beaten wheat, and twenty thousand measures of barley, and twenty thousand baths of wine, and twenty thousand baths of oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> Then Huram &#91;noble born&#93; the king of Tzor (Tyre) &#91;a rock&#93; answered in writing, which he sent to Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, Because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has loved his people, he has made youi king over them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> Huram &#91;noble born&#93; said moreover, Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that made heaven and earth, who has given to David &#91;beloved&#93; the king a wise son, endued with prudence and understanding, that might build an house for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and an house for his kingdom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And now I have sent a cunning man, endued with understanding, of Huram &#91;noble born&#93; my father’s,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> The son of a woman of the daughters of Dan &#91;Judge&#93;, and his father &#91;was&#93; a man of Tzor &#91;a rock&#93;, skilful to work in gold, and in silver, in brass, in iron, in stone, and in timber, in purple, in blue, and in fine linen, and in crimson; also to grave any manner of graving, and to find out every device which shall be put to him, with youi cunning men, and with the cunning men of my lord David &#91;beloved&#93; youi father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; numbered all the strangers that &#91;were&#93; in the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, after the numbering which David &#91;beloved&#93; his father had numbered them; and they were found an hundred and fifty thousand and three thousand and six hundred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

And Huram &#91;noble born&#93; finished the work that he was to make for king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; for the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> &#91;To wit&#93;, the two pillars, and the pommels (bowl-shaped top of a pillar), and the columns &#91;which were&#93; on the top of the two pillars, and the two wreaths to cover the two pommels (bowl-shaped top of a pillar) of the columns which &#91;were&#93; on the top of the pillars;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And four hundred pomegranates on the two wreaths; two rows of pomegranates on each wreath, to cover the two pommels (bowl-shaped top of a pillar) of the columns which &#91;were&#93; upon the pillars.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> He made also bases, and lavers made he upon the bases;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> One sea, and twelve oxen under it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> The pots also, and the shovels, and the fleshhooks, and all their instruments, did Huram &#91;noble born&#93; his father make to king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; for the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) of bright brass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> In the plain of Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; did the king cast them, in the clay ground between Sukkot &#91;Booths&#93; and Tz’redah &#91;fortress&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Thus Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; made all these vessels in great abundance: for the weight of the brass could not be found out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; made all the vessels that &#91;were for&#93; the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the golden altar also, and the tables whereon the showbread &#91;was set&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Moreover the candlesticks with their lamps, that they should burn after the manner before the oracle, of pure gold;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And the flowers, and the lamps, and the tongs, &#91;made he of&#93; gold, &#91;and&#93; that perfect gold;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And the snuffers, and the basons, and the spoons, and the censers, &#91;of&#93; pure gold: and the entry of the house, the inner doors thereof for the most holy &#91;place&#93;, and the doors of the house of the temple, &#91;were of&#93; gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Thus all the work that Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; made for the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was finished: and Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; brought in &#91;all&#93; the things that David &#91;beloved&#93; his father had dedicated; and the silver, and the gold, and all the instruments, put he among the treasures of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> Then Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; assembled the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and all the heads of the tribes, the chief of the fathers of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, unto Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, to bring up the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) out of the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> For what reason all the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; assembled themselves unto the king in the feast which &#91;was&#93; in the seventh month.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> And all the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; came; and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; took up the ark.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And they brought up the ark, and the tabernacle of the congregation, and all the holy vessels that &#91;were&#93; in the tabernacle, these did the priests &#91;and&#93; the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; bring up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> Also king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, and all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that were assembled unto him before the ark, sacrificed sheep and oxen, which could not be told nor numbered for multitude.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> And the priests brought in the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto his place, to the oracle of the house, into the most holy &#91;place, even&#93; under the wings of the k’vurim (cherubim):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> For the k’vurim (cherubim) spread forth &#91;their&#93; wings over the place of the ark, and the k’vurim (cherubim) covered the ark and the rods (clubs) thereof above.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> And they drew out the rods (clubs) &#91;of the ark&#93;, that the ends of the rods (clubs) were seen from the ark before the oracle; but they were not seen without. And there it is unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> &#91;There was&#93; nothing in the ark save the two tables which Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; put &#91;in it&#93; at Horev &#91;Desert&#93;, when the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) made &#91;a covenant&#93; with the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when they came out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> And it came to pass, when the priests were come out of the holy &#91;place&#93; (for all the priests &#91;that were&#93; present were sanctified, &#91;and&#93; did not &#91;then&#93; wait by course):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> Also the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;which were&#93; the singers, all of them of Asaf &#91;gatherer&#93;, of Heiman &#91;faithful&#93;, of Y’dutun &#91;praising&#93;, with their sons and their brethren, &#91;being&#93; arrayed in white linen, having cymbals and psalteries and harps, stood at the east end of the altar, and with them an hundred and twenty priests sounding with trumpets</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> It came even to pass, as the trumpeters and singers &#91;were&#93; as one, to make one sound to be heard in praising and thanking the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and when they lifted up &#91;their&#93; voice with the trumpets and cymbals and instruments of music, and praised the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;saying&#93;, For &#91;he is&#93; good; for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever: that &#91;then&#93; the house was filled with a cloud, &#91;even&#93; the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate);</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> So that the priests could not stand to minister by reason of the cloud: for the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had filled the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Then said Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has said that he would dwell in the thick darkness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> But I have built an house of habitation for youi, and a place for youi dwelling forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> And the king turned his face, and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the whole congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; stood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> And he said, Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, who has with his hands fulfilled &#91;that&#93; which he spoke with his mouth to my father David &#91;beloved&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> Since the day that I brought forth my people out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; I chose no city among all the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to build an house in, that my name might be there; neither chose I any man to be a ruler over my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> But I have chosen Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, that my name might be there; and have chosen David &#91;beloved&#93; to be over my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> Now it was in the heart of David &#91;beloved&#93; my father to build an house for the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> But the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said to David &#91;beloved&#93; my father, Forasmuch as it was in youi heart to build an house for my name, youi did well in that it was in youi heart:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> Yet youi shall not build the house; but youi son which shall come forth out of youi waist, he shall build the house for my name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) therefore has performed his word that he has spoken: for I am risen up in the room of David &#91;beloved&#93; my father, and am set on the throne of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) promised, and have built the house for the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> And in it have I put the ark, wherein &#91;is&#93; the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that he made with the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> And he stood before the altar of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the presence of all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and spread forth his hands:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> For Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; had made a brasen scaffold, of five cubits long, and five cubits broad, and three cubits high, and had set it in the midst of the court: and upon it he stood, and kneeled down upon his knees before all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and spread forth his hands toward heaven,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> And said, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, &#91;there is&#93; no God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; like youi in the heaven, nor in the earth; which keeps covenant, and &#91;shows&#93; mercy unto youi servants, that walk before youi with all their hearts:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> youi which have kept with youri servant David &#91;beloved&#93; my father that which youi have promised him; and spoke with youri mouth, and have fulfilled &#91;it&#93; with youri hand, as &#91;it is&#93; this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> Now therefore, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, keep with youi servant David &#91;beloved&#93; my father that which youi have promised him, saying, There shall not fail youi a man in my sight to sit upon the throne of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; yet so that youri children take heed to their way to walk in my law, as youi have walked before me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> Now then, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, let youri word be verified, which youi have spoken unto youri servant David &#91;beloved&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> But will God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in very deed dwell with men on the earth? behold, heaven and the heaven of heavens cannot contain youi; how much less this house which I have built!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Have respect therefore to the prayer of youri servant, and to his supplication, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, to hearken unto the cry and the prayer which youri servant prays before youi:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> That youri eyes may be open upon this house day and night, upon the place whereof youi have said that youi would put youri name there; to hearken unto the prayer which youri servant prays toward this place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Listen therefore unto the supplications of youri servant, and of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which they shall make toward this place: hear youi from youri dwelling place, &#91;even&#93; from heaven; and when youi hear, forgive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> If a man sin against his neighbour, and an oath be laid upon him to make him swear, and the oath come before youri altar in this house;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Then hear youi from heaven, and do, and judge youri servants, by requiting the wicked, by recompensing his way upon his own head; and by justifying the righteous, by giving him according to his righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And if youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; be put to the worse before the enemy, because they have sinned against youi; and shall return and confess youri name, and pray and make supplication before youi in this house;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Then hear youi from the heavens, and forgive the sin of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and bring them again unto the land which youi gave to them and to their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> When the heaven is shut up, and there is no rain, because they have sinned against youi; &#91;yet&#93; if they pray toward this place, and confess youri name, and turn from their sin, when youi do afflict them;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Then hear youi from heaven, and forgive the sin of youri servants, and of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when youi have taught them the good way, wherein they should walk; and send rain upon youi land, which youi have given unto youri people for an inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> If there be famine in the land, if there be pestilence, if there be blasting, or mildew, locusts, or caterpillers; if their enemies besiege them in the cities of their land; whatsoever sore or whatsoever sickness &#91;there be&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> &#91;Then&#93; what prayer &#91;or&#93; what supplication whatsoever shall be made of any man, or of all youi people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when every one shall know his own sore and his own grief, and shall spread forth his hands in this house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Then hear youi from heaven youri dwelling place, and forgive, and render unto every man according unto all his ways, whose heart youi know; (for youi only know the hearts of the children of men)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> That they may fear youi, to walk in youri ways, so long as they live in the land which youi gave unto our fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Moreover concerning the stranger, which is not of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, but is come from a far country for youri great name’s sake, and youri mighty hand, and youri stretched out arm; if they come and pray in this house;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Then hear youi from the heavens, &#91;even&#93; from youri dwelling place, and do according to all that the stranger calls to youi for; that all people of the earth may know youri name, and fear youi, as &#91;does&#93; youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and may know that this house which I have built is called by youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> If youi people go out to war against their enemies by the way that youi shall send them, and they pray unto youi toward this city which youi have chosen, and the house which I have built for youri name;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Then hear youi from the heavens their prayer and their supplication, and maintain their cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> If they sin against youi, (for &#91;there is&#93; no man which sins not,) and youi be angry with them, and deliver them over before &#91;their&#93; enemies, and they carry them away captives unto a land far off or near;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Yet &#91;if&#93; they reconsider themselves in the land to which they are carried captive, and turn and pray unto youi in the land of their captivity, saying, We have sinned, we have done amiss (wrong; incorrect), and have dealt wickedly;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> If they return to youi with all their heart and with all their soul in the land of their captivity, to which they have carried them captives, and pray toward their land, which youi gave unto their fathers, and &#91;toward&#93; the city which youi have chosen, and toward the house which I have built for youri name:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> Then hear youi from the heavens, &#91;even&#93; from youri dwelling place, their prayer and their supplications, and maintain their cause, and forgive youri people which have sinned against youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> Now, my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, let, I beseech (to call upon; appeal; beg) youi, youri eyes be open, and &#91;let&#93; youri ears &#91;be&#93; attentive unto the prayer &#91;that is made&#93; in this place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> Now therefore arise, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, into youri resting place, youi, and the ark of youri strength: let youri priests, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, be clothed with salvation, and let youi saints rejoice in goodness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, turn not away the face of youi anointed: remember the mercies of David &#91;beloved&#93; youi servant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And the priests could not enter into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), because the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had filled the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; house.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> For now have I chosen and sanctified this house, that my name may be there forever: and my eyes and my heart shall be there perpetually.</span>

<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> And as for youi, if youi will walk before me, as David &#91;beloved&#93; youi father walked, and do according to all that I have commanded youi, and shall observe my statutes and my judgments;</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Then will I pluck them up by the roots out of my land which I have given them; and this house, which I have sanctified for my name, will I cast out of my sight, and will make it &#91;to be&#93; a proverb and a byword among all nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And it shall be answered, Because they forsook the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers, which brought them forth out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and laid hold on other gods, and worshipped them, and served them: therefore has he brought all this evil upon them.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> For the king’s ships went to Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93; with the servants of Huram &#91;noble born&#93;: every three years once came the ships of Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93; bringing gold, and silver, ivory, and apes, and peacocks.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Then Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; the son of Kena’anah &#91;trader&#93; came near, and struck Mikhay’hu &#91;who is like God?&#93; upon the cheek, and said, Which way went the Spirit of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) from me to speak unto youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And Mikhay’hu &#91;who is like God?&#93; said, Behold, youi shall see on that day when youi shall go into an inner chamber to hide yourself.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Then the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; said, Take you2f Mikhay’hu &#91;who is like God?&#93;, and carry him back to ‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93; the governor of the city, and to Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93; the king’s son;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And say, Thus says the king, Put &#91;a bow&#93; this &#91;fellow&#93; in the prison, and feed him with bread of affliction and with water of affliction, until I return in peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And Mikhay’hu &#91;who is like God?&#93; said, If youi certainly return in peace, &#91;then&#93; has not the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoken by me. And he said, Listen, all you2f people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> So the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; went up to Ramot-Gil’ad &#91;rocky region&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; said unto Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, I will disguise myself, and will go to the battle; but put youi on youi robes. So the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; disguised himself; and they went to the battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Now the king of Aram &#91;exalted&#93; (Syria) &#91;exalted&#93; had commanded the captains of the chariots that &#91;were&#93; with him, saying, Fight you2f not with small or great, save only with the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And it came to pass, when the captains of the chariots saw Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, that they said, It &#91;is&#93; the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;. Therefore they compassed about him to fight: but Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; cried out, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) helped him; and God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; moved them &#91;to depart&#93; from him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> For it came to pass, that, when the captains of the chariots perceived that it was not the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, they turned back again from pursuing him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And a &#91;certain&#93; man drew a bow at a venture, and struck the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; between the joints of the harness: therefore he said to his chariot man, Turn youi hand, that youi may carry me out of the host; for I am wounded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And the battle increased that day: howbeit the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; detained (held) &#91;himself&#93; up in &#91;his&#93; chariot against the Aram &#91;exalted&#93; &#91;Syrians&#93; until the even: and about the time of the sun going down he died.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; returned to his house in peace to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And Yehu &#91;Yehovah is He&#93; the son of Hanani &#91;gracious&#93; the seer went out to meet him, and said to king Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, Should youi help the ungodly, and love them that hate the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate)? therefore &#91;is&#93; wrath upon youi from before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> Nevertheless there are good things found in youi, in that youi have taken away the Asherot out of the land, and have prepared youri heart to seek God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; dwelt at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: and he went out again through the people from Be’er-sheva &#91;Well of the sevenfold Oath&#93; to mount Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and brought them back unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And he set in the land throughout all the fenced cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, city by city,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> And said to the judges, Take heed what you2f do: for you2f judge not for man, but for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), who &#91;is&#93; with you in the judgment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> For what reason now let the fear of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) be upon you; take heed and do &#91;it&#93;: for &#91;there is&#93; no iniquity with the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, nor respect of persons, nor taking of gifts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> And what cause whosoever shall come to you of your brethren that dwell in their cities, between blood and blood, between law and commandment, statutes and judgments, you2f shall even warn them that they trespass not against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and &#91;so&#93; wrath come upon you, and upon your brethren: this do, and you2f shall not trespass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> And, behold, Amaryah &#91;Yehovah speaks&#93; the chief priest &#91;is&#93; over you in all matters of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and Z’vadyah &#91;endowment of Yehovah&#93; the son of Yishma’el &#91;God will hear&#93;, the ruler of the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, for all the king’s matters: also the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;shall be&#93; officers before you. Deal courageously, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall be with the good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> It came to pass after this also, &#91;that&#93; the children of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, and the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, and with them &#91;other&#93; beside the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, came against Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; to battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> Then there came some that told Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, saying, There comes a great multitude against youi from beyond the sea on this side Aram &#91;exalted&#93; (Syria) &#91;exalted&#93;; and, behold, they &#91;be&#93; in Hatzatzon-Tamar &#91;dividing the date-palm&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; ‘Ein-Gedi &#91;Fount of the Kid&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; feared, and set himself to seek the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and proclaimed a fast throughout all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; stood in the congregation of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), before the new court,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> And said, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of our fathers, &#91;are&#93; not youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in heaven? and rule &#91;not&#93; youi over all the kingdoms of the heathen? and in youri hand &#91;is there not&#93; power and might, so that none is able that is to withstand youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> &#91;Are&#93; not youi our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;who&#93; did drive out the inhabitants of this land before youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and gave it to the seed of Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93; youri friend forever?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> And they dwelt in it, and have built youi a sanctuary in it for youri name, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> If, &#91;when&#93; evil comes upon us, &#91;as&#93; the sword, judgment, or pestilence, or famine, we stand before this house, and in youri presence, (for youri name &#91;is&#93; in this house,) and cry unto youi in our affliction, then youi will hear and help.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> And now, behold, the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93; and Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; and mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, whom youi would not let Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; invade, when they came out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, but they turned from them, and destroyed them not;</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> O our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, will youi not judge them? for we have no might against this great company that comes against us; neither know we what to do: but our eyes &#91;are&#93; upon youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; stood before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), with their little ones, their wives, and their children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> Then upon Yachazi’el &#91;beheld of God&#93; the son of Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93;, the son of B’nayahu &#91;Yehovah has built&#93;, the son of Ye’i’el &#91;God sweeps away&#93;, the son of Matanyah &#91;gift of Yehovah&#93;, a Levi (Levite) &#91;Joined to&#93; of the sons of Asaf &#91;gatherer&#93;, came the Spirit of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the midst of the congregation;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And he said, Listen you2f, all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and you2f inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and youi king Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto you, Be not afraid nor dismayed by reason of this great multitude; for the battle &#91;is&#93; not yours, but God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;’s.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> Tomorrow go you2f down against them: behold, they come up by the cliff of Tzitz &#91;flower&#93;; and you2f shall find them at the end of the brook, before the wilderness of Yeru’el &#91;taught by God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> You2f shall not &#91;need&#93; to fight in this &#91;battle&#93;: set yourselves, stand you2f &#91;still&#93;, and see the salvation of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) with you, O Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: fear not, nor be dismayed; tomorrow go out against them: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;will be&#93; with you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; bowed his head with &#91;his&#93; face to the ground: and all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; fell before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), worshipping the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, of the children of the K’hati, and of the children of the Korchi &#91;of baldness&#93;, stood up to praise the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; with a loud voice on high.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And they rose early in the morning, and went forth into the wilderness of T’koa &#91;a stockade&#93;: and as they went forth, Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; stood and said, Hear me, O Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and you2f inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;; Believe in the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, so shall you2f be established; believe his prophets, so shall you2f prosper.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And when he had consulted with the people, he appointed singers unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and that should praise the beauty of holiness, as they went out before the army, and to say, Praise the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And when they began to sing and to praise, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) set ambushes against the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, and mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, which were come against Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; and they were smitten.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> For the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93; and Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; stood up against the inhabitants of mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, utterly to slay and destroy &#91;them&#93;: and when they had made an end of the inhabitants of Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, every one helped to destroy another.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And when Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; came toward the watch tower in the wilderness, they looked unto the multitude, and, behold, they &#91;were&#93; dead bodies fallen to the earth, and none escaped.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And when Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; and his people came to take away the spoil (plunder; prey; booty) of them, they found among them in abundance both riches with the dead bodies, and precious jewels, which they stripped off for themselves, more than they could carry away: and they were three days in gathering of the spoil (plunder; prey; booty), it was so much.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And on the fourth day they assembled themselves in the valley of B’rakhah &#91;blessing&#93;; for there they Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): therefore the name of the same place was called, The valley of B’rakhah &#91;blessing&#93;, unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Then they returned, every man of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; in the forefront of them, to go again to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; with joy; for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had made them to rejoice over their enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And they came to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; with psalteries and harps and trumpets unto the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And the fear of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; was on all the kingdoms of &#91;those&#93; countries, when they had heard that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) fought against the enemies of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> So the realm of Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; was quiet: for his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; gave him rest round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; reigned over Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;: &#91;he was&#93; thirty and five years old when he began to reign, and he reigned twenty and five years in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;. And his mother’s name &#91;was&#93; ‘Azuvah &#91;forsaken&#93; the daughter of Shilchi &#91;armed&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And he walked in the way of Asa &#91;healer; injurious?&#93; his father, and departed not from it, doing &#91;that which was&#93; right in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> However the high places were not taken away: for as yet the people had not prepared their hearts unto the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Now the rest of the acts of Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, first and last, behold, they &#91;are&#93; written in the book of Yehu &#91;Yehovah is He&#93; the son of Hanani &#91;gracious&#93;, who &#91;is&#93; mentioned in the book of the kings of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And after this did Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; join himself with Achazyah &#91;Yehovah possesses&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, who did very wickedly:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And he joined himself with him to make ships to go to Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93;: and they made the ships in ‘Etzyon-Gever &#91;backbone of a man&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> Then Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; took advice, and sent to Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93;, the son of Y’ho’achaz &#91;Yehovah has seized&#93;, the son of Yehu &#91;Yehovah is He&#93;, king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, Come, let us see one another in the face.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> And Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; sent to Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, saying, The thistle that &#91;was&#93; in L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93; sent to the cedar that &#91;was&#93; in L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;, saying, Give youi daughter to my son to wife: and there passed by a wild beast that &#91;was&#93; in L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;, and trampled down the thistle.</span>

<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> youi say, Lo, youi have smitten the Edomi &#91;red&#93;; and youi heart lifts youi up to boast: abide now at home; why should youi meddle to &#91;youi&#93; hurt, that youi should fall, &#91;even&#93; youi, and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; with youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> So Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93; the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; went up; and they saw one another in the face, &#91;both&#93; he and Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, at Beit-Shemesh &#91;House of the Sun&#93;, which &#91;belongs&#93; to Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; was put to the worse before Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and they fled every man to his tent.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93; the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; took Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, the son of Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93;, the son of Y’ho’achaz &#91;Yehovah has seized&#93;, at Beit-Shemesh &#91;House of the Sun&#93;, and brought him to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and broke down the wall of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; from the gate of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; to the corner gate, four hundred cubits.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And &#91;he took&#93; all the gold and the silver, and all the vessels that were found in the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; with ‘Oved-Edom &#91;servant of Edom&#93;, and the treasures of the king’s house, the hostages also, and returned to Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93; the son of Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; lived after the death of Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93; son of Y’ho’achaz &#91;Yehovah has seized&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; fifteen years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Now the rest of the acts of Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93;, first and last, behold, &#91;are&#93; they not written in the book of the kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Now after the time that Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93; did turn away from following the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) they made a conspiracy against him in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;; and he fled to Lakhish &#91;Invincible&#93;: but they sent to Lakhish &#91;Invincible&#93; after him, and killed him there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Then all the people of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; took ‘Uziyah &#91;my strength is Yehovah&#93;, who &#91;was&#93; sixteen years old, and made him king in the room of his father Amatzyah &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> For again the Edomi &#91;red&#93; had come and smitten Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and carried away captives.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> For the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; low because of Achaz &#91;he has grasped&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; for he made Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; naked, and transgressed sore against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And Tigat-Piln’eser &#91;you will uncover the wonderful bond&#93; king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))) came unto him, and distressed him, but strengthened him not.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And Achaz &#91;he has grasped&#93; gathered together the vessels of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and cut in pieces the vessels of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and shut up the doors of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and he made him altars in every corner of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And in every single (each one separately) city of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; he made high places to burn incense unto other gods, and provoked to anger the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of his fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Now the rest of his acts and of all his ways, first and last, behold, they &#91;are&#93; written in the book of the kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And Achaz &#91;he has grasped&#93; slept with his fathers, and they buried him in the city, &#91;even&#93; in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: but they brought him not into the sepulchres of the kings of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; his son reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; began to reign &#91;when he was&#93; five and twenty years old, and he reigned nine and twenty years in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;. And his mother’s name &#91;was&#93; Aviyah &#91;Yehovah is (my) father&#93;, the daughter of Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And he did &#91;that which was&#93; right in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), according to all that David &#91;beloved&#93; his father had done.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> He in the first year of his reign, in the first month, opened the doors of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and repaired them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And said unto them, Hear me, you2f Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) now yourselves, and sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of your fathers, and carry forth the filthiness out of the holy &#91;place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> For our fathers have trespassed, and done &#91;that which was&#93; evil in the eyes of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and have forsaken him, and have turned away their faces from the habitation of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and turned &#91;their&#93; backs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> Also they have shut up the doors of the porch, and put out the lamps, and have not burned incense nor offered burnt offerings in the holy &#91;place&#93; unto the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> For what reason the wrath of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was upon Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and he has delivered them to trouble, to astonishment, and to hissing, as you2f see with your eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> For, lo, our fathers have fallen by the sword, and our sons and our daughters and our wives &#91;are&#93; in captivity for this.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> Now &#91;it is&#93; in my heart to make a covenant with the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that his fierce wrath may turn away from us.</span>
