<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> My sons, be not now negligent: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has chosen you to stand before him, to serve him, and that you2f should minister unto him, and burn incense.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> Then the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; arose, Machat &#91;grasping&#93; the son of ‘Amasai &#91;burdensome&#93;, and Yo’el &#91;Yehovah is God&#93; the son of ‘Azaryah &#91;Yehovah has helped&#93;, of the sons of the K’hati: and of the sons of M’rari &#91;Bitter&#93;, Kish &#91;bent&#93; the son of ‘Abdi &#91;servant of Yehovah&#93;, and ‘Azaryah &#91;Yehovah has helped&#93; the son of Yehallel’el &#91;God is praised&#93;: and of the Gershuni &#91;Exiled Ones&#93;; Yo’ach &#91;Yahovah is brother&#93; the son of Zimah &#91;plotter&#93;, and ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93; the son of Yo’ach &#91;Yahovah is brother&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And of the sons of Elitzafan &#91;My God has Protected&#93;; Shimri &#91;vigilant&#93;, and Ye’i’el &#91;God sweeps away&#93;: and of the sons of Asaf &#91;gatherer&#93;; Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93;, and Matanyah &#91;gift of Yehovah&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> And of the sons of Heiman &#91;faithful&#93;; Jehiel, and Shime’i &#91;Fame&#93;: and of the sons of Y’dutun &#91;praising&#93;; Sh’may’yah &#91;Yehovah hears&#93;, and Uzziel &#91;My Strength is God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And they gathered their brethren, and sanctified themselves, and came, according to the commandment of the king, by the words of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to cleanse the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> And the priests went into the inner part of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to cleanse &#91;it&#93;, and brought out all the uncleanness that they found in the temple of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) into the court of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). And the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; took &#91;it&#93;, to carry &#91;it&#93; out abroad into the brook Kidron &#91;dark&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> Now they began on the first &#91;day&#93; of the first month to sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use), and on the eighth day of the month came they to the porch of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): so they sanctified the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in eight days; and in the sixteenth day of the first month they made an end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Then they went in to Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; the king, and said, We have cleansed all the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and the altar of burnt offering, with all the vessels thereof, and the showbread table, with all the vessels thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Moreover all the vessels, which king Achaz &#91;he has grasped&#93; in his reign did cast away in his transgression, have we prepared and sanctified, and, behold, they &#91;are&#93; before the altar of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Then Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; the king rose early, and gathered the rulers of the city, and went up to the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And they brought seven bullocks, and seven rams, and seven lambs, and seven he goats, for a sin offering for the kingdom, and for the sanctuary, and for Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;. And he commanded the priests the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; to offer &#91;them&#93; on the altar of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> So they killed the bullocks, and the priests received the blood, and sprinkled &#91;it&#93; on the altar: likewise, when they had killed the rams, they sprinkled the blood upon the altar: they killed also the lambs, and they sprinkled the blood upon the altar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And they brought forth the he goats &#91;for&#93; the sin offering before the king and the congregation; and they laid their hands upon them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And the priests killed them, and they made reconciliation with their blood upon the altar, to make an atonement for all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: for the king commanded &#91;that&#93; the burnt offering and the sin offering &#91;should be made&#93; for all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And he set the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) with cymbals, with psalteries, and with harps, according to the commandment of David &#91;beloved&#93;, and of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; the king’s seer, and Natan &#91;giver&#93; the prophet: for &#91;so was&#93; the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) by his prophets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; stood with the instruments of David &#91;beloved&#93;, and the priests with the trumpets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; commanded to offer the burnt offering upon the altar. And when the burnt offering began, the song of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) began &#91;also&#93; with the trumpets, and with the instruments &#91;ordained&#93; by David &#91;beloved&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And all the congregation worshipped, and the singers sang, and the trumpeters sounded: &#91;and&#93; all &#91;this continued&#93; until the burnt offering was finished.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And when they had made an end of offering, the king and all that were present with him bowed themselves, and worshipped.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Moreover Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; the king and the princes commanded the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; to sing praise unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) with the words of David &#91;beloved&#93;, and of Asaf &#91;gatherer&#93; the seer. And they sang praises with gladness, and they bowed their heads and worshipped.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Then Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; answered and said, Now you2f have consecrated yourselves unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), come near and bring sacrifices and thank offerings into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). And the congregation brought in sacrifices and thank offerings; and as many as were of a free heart burnt offerings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And the number of the burnt offerings, which the congregation brought, was threescore and ten bullocks, an hundred rams, &#91;and&#93; two hundred lambs: all these &#91;were&#93; for a burnt offering to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And the consecrated things &#91;were&#93; six hundred oxen and three thousand sheep.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> But the priests were too few, so that they could not flay (stip the skin off) all the burnt offerings: wherefore their brethren the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; did help them, till the work was ended, and until the &#91;other&#93; priests had sanctified themselves: for the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;were&#93; more upright in heart to sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) themselves than the priests.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And also the burnt offerings &#91;were&#93; in abundance, with the fat of the peace offerings, and the drink offerings for &#91;every&#93; burnt offering. So the service of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was set in order.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; rejoiced, and all the people, that God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; had prepared the people: for the thing was &#91;done&#93; suddenly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; sent to all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and wrote letters also to Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; and M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, that they should come to the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, to keep the Passover unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> For the king had taken counsel, and his princes, and all the congregation in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, to keep the Passover in the second month.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> For they could not keep it at that time, because the priests had not sanctified themselves sufficiently, neither had the people gathered themselves together to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> And the thing pleased the king and all the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> So they established a decree to make proclamation throughout all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, from Be’er-sheva &#91;Well of the sevenfold Oath&#93; even to Dan &#91;Judge&#93;, that they should come to keep the Passover unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: for they had not done &#91;it&#93; of a long &#91;time in such sort&#93; as it was written.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> So the posts went with the letters from the king and his princes throughout all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and according to the commandment of the king, saying, You2f people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, turn again unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and he will return to the remnant of you, that are escaped out of the hand of the kings of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> And be not you2f like your fathers, and like your brethren, which trespassed against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers, &#91;who&#93; therefore gave them up to desolation, as you2f see.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> Now be you2f not stiffnecked, as your fathers &#91;were, but&#93; yield yourselves unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and enter into his sanctuary, which he has sanctified forever: and serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, that the fierceness of his wrath may turn away from you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> For if you2f turn again unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), your brethren and your children &#91;shall find&#93; compassion before them that lead them captive, so that they shall come again into this land: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; gracious and merciful, and will not turn away &#91;his&#93; face from you, if you2f return unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> So the posts passed from city to city through the country of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; and M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; even unto Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;: but they laughed them to scorn, and mocked them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> Nevertheless diverse of Asher &#91;Happy&#93; and M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; and of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93; humbled themselves, and came to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> Also in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; the hand of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; was to give them one heart to do the commandment of the king and of the princes, by the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And there assembled at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; much people to keep the feast of unleavened bread in the second month, a very great congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> And they arose and took away the altars that &#91;were&#93; in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and all the altars for incense took they away, and cast &#91;them&#93; into the brook Kidron &#91;dark&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> Then they killed the Passover on the fourteenth &#91;day&#93; of the second month: and the priests and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; were ashamed, and sanctified themselves, and brought in the burnt offerings into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> And they stood in their place after their manner, according to the law of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; the man of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: the priests sprinkled the blood, &#91;which they received&#93; of the hand of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> For &#91;there were&#93; many in the congregation that were not sanctified: therefore the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; had the charge of the killing of the passovers for every one &#91;that was&#93; not clean, to sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) &#91;them&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> For a multitude of the people, &#91;even&#93; many of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, and Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, had not cleansed themselves, yet did they eat the Passover otherwise than it was written. But Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; prayed for them, saying, The good LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) pardon every one</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> &#91;That&#93; prepares his heart to seek God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of his fathers, though &#91;he be&#93; not &#91;cleansed&#93; according to the purification of the sanctuary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) listened to Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93;, and healed the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that were present at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; kept the feast of unleavened bread seven days with great gladness: and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; and the priests praised the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) day by day, &#91;singing&#93; with loud instruments unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; spoke comfortably unto all the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; that taught the good knowledge of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and they did eat throughout the feast seven days, offering peace offerings, and making confession to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And the whole assembly took counsel to keep other seven days: and they kept &#91;other&#93; seven days with gladness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> For Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; did give to the congregation a thousand bullocks and seven thousand sheep; and the princes gave to the congregation a thousand bullocks and ten thousand sheep: and a great number of priests sanctified themselves.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And all the congregation of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, with the priests and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, and all the congregation that came out of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and the strangers that came out of the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and that dwelt in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, rejoiced.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> So there was great joy in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: for since the time of Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; the son of David &#91;beloved&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;there was&#93; not the like in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Then the priests the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; arose and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the people: and their voice was heard, and their prayer came &#91;up&#93; to his holy dwelling place, &#91;even&#93; unto heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Now when all this was finished, all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that were present went out to the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and broke the images in pieces, and cut down the groves, and threw down the high places and the altars out of all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, in Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; also and M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, until they had utterly destroyed them all. Then all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; returned, every man to his possession, into their own cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; appointed the courses of the priests and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; after their courses, every man according to his service, the priests and Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; for burnt offerings and for peace offerings, to minister, and to give thanks, and to praise in the gates of the tents of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> Moreover he commanded the people that dwelt in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; to give the portion of the priests and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, that they might be encouraged in the law of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And as soon as the commandment came abroad, the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; brought in abundance the firstfruits of corn, wine, and oil, and honey, and of all the increase of the field; and the tithe of all &#91;things&#93; brought they in abundantly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> And &#91;concerning&#93; the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, that dwelt in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, they also brought in the tithe of oxen and sheep, and the tithe of holy things which were consecrated unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and laid &#91;them&#93; by heaps.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And Jehiel, and ‘Azazyahu &#91;Yehovah is mighty&#93;, and Nachat &#91;rest&#93;, and ‘Asah’el &#91;God-made&#93;, and Yerimot &#91;He is Most High&#93;, and Yozavad &#91;Yehovah has endowed&#93;, and Eli’el &#91;my God is God&#93;, and Yishmachyah &#91;Yehovah sustains&#93;, and Machat &#91;grasping&#93;, and B’nayahu &#91;Yehovah has built&#93;, &#91;were&#93; overseers under the hand of Konanyahu &#91;Yehovah has established&#93; and Shime’i &#91;Fame&#93; his brother, at the commandment of Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; the king, and ‘Azaryah &#91;Yehovah has helped&#93; the ruler of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> And Kore &#91;crier&#93; the son of Yimnah &#91;right hand&#93; the Levi (Levite) &#91;Joined to&#93;, the porter toward the east, &#91;was&#93; over the freewill offerings of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, to distribute the oblations of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and the most holy things.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And next him &#91;were&#93; ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93;, and Minyamin &#91;from the right hand&#93;, and Yeshua &#91;he is saved&#93;, and Sh’may’yah &#91;Yehovah hears&#93;, Amaryah &#91;Yehovah speaks&#93;, and Sh’khanyahu &#91;dweller with Yehovah&#93;, in the cities of the priests, in &#91;their&#93; set office, to give to their brethren by courses, as well to the great as to the small:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> Beside their genealogy of males, from three years old and upward, &#91;even&#93; unto every one that enters into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), his daily portion for their service in their charges according to their courses;</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Also of the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priests, &#91;which were&#93; in the fields of the suburbs of their cities, in every single (each one separately) city, the men that were expressed by name, to give portions to all the males among the priests, and to all that were reckoned by genealogies among the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And thus did Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; throughout all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and wrought &#91;that which was&#93; good and right and tbefore the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And in every work that he began in the service of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and in the law, and in the commandments, to seek his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, he did &#91;it&#93; with all his heart, and prospered.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> After these things, and the establishment thereof, Sancheriv &#91;Sin (the moon) multiplied brothers&#93; king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))) came, and entered into Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and encamped against the fenced cities, and thought to win them for himself.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And when Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; saw that Sancheriv &#91;Sin (the moon) multiplied brothers&#93; was come, and that he was purposed to fight against Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> So there was gathered much people together, who stopped all the fountains, and the brook that ran through the midst of the land, saying, Why should the kings of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))) come, and find much water?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> Also he strengthened himself, and built up all the wall that was broken, and raised &#91;it&#93; up to the towers, and another wall without, and repaired Millo &#91;fortification (defensive structure) or mound&#93; &#91;in&#93; the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;, and made darts and shields in abundance.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> Be strong and courageous, be not afraid nor dismayed for the king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))), nor for all the multitude that &#91;is&#93; with him: for &#91;there be&#93; more with us than with him:</span>
</div>
