And the people rested themselves upon the words of Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> After this did Sancheriv &#91;Sin (the moon) multiplied brothers&#93; king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))) send his servants to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, (but he &#91;himself laid siege&#93; against Lakhish &#91;Invincible&#93;, and all his power with him,) unto Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and unto all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; that &#91;were&#93; at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> Thus says Sancheriv &#91;Sin (the moon) multiplied brothers&#93; king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))), Whereon do you2f trust, that you2f abide in the siege in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> Does not Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; persuade you to give over yourselves to die by famine and by thirst, saying, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; shall deliver us out of the hand of the king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known)))?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> Has not the same Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; taken away his high places and his altars, and commanded Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, saying, You2f shall worship before one altar, and burn incense upon it?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> Know you2f not what I and my fathers have done unto all the people of &#91;other&#93; lands? were the gods of the nations of those lands any ways able to deliver their lands out of my hand?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> Who &#91;was there&#93; among all the gods of those nations that my fathers utterly destroyed, that could deliver his people out of my hand, that your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; should be able to deliver you out of my hand?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> Now therefore let not Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; deceive you, nor persuade you on this manner, neither yet believe him: for no god of any nation or kingdom was able to deliver his people out of my hand, and out of the hand of my fathers: how much less shall your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; deliver you out of my hand?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> And his servants spoke yet &#91;more&#93; against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and against his servant Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> He wrote also letters to rail on the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and to speak against him, saying, As the gods of the nations of &#91;other&#93; lands have not delivered their people out of my hand, so shall not the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; deliver his people out of my hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Then they cried with a loud voice in the Y’hudit (Y’hudim (Judeans)’) speech unto the people of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; that &#91;were&#93; on the wall, to affright them, and to trouble them; that they might take the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And they spoke against the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, as against the gods of the people of the earth, &#91;which were&#93; the work of the hands of man.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And for this &#91;cause&#93; Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; the king, and the prophet Yesha’yahu &#91;Yehovah has saved&#93; the son of Amotz &#91;strong&#93;, prayed and cried to heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) sent an angel, which cut off all the mighty men of valour, and the leaders and captains in the camp of the king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))). So he returned with shame of face to his own land. And when he was come into the house of his god, they that came forth of his own bowels (inward parts; affections) killed him there with the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Thus the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) saved Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; from the hand of Sancheriv &#91;Sin (the moon) multiplied brothers&#93; the king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))), and from the hand of all &#91;other&#93;, and guided them on every side.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And many brought gifts unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and presents to Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;: so that he was magnified in the sight of all nations from thenceforth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> In those days Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; was sick to the death, and prayed unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and he spoke unto him, and he gave him a sign.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> But Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; rendered not again according to the benefit &#91;done&#93; unto him; for his heart was lifted up: therefore there was wrath upon him, and upon Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Yet Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; humbled himself for the pride of his heart, &#91;both&#93; he and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, so that the wrath of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came not upon them in the days of Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; had exceeding much riches and honour: and he made himself treasuries for silver, and for gold, and for precious stones, and for spices, and for shields, and for all manner of pleasant jewels;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Storehouses also for the increase of corn, and wine, and oil; and stalls for all manner of beasts, and stables for flocks.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Moreover he provided him cities, and possessions of flocks and herds in abundance: for God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; had given him substance very much.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> This same Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; also stopped the upper watercourse of Gikhon (Bursting Forth), and brought it straight down to the west side of the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;. And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; prospered in all his works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> However in &#91;the business of&#93; the ambassadors of the princes of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, who sent unto him to enquire of the wonder that was &#91;done&#93; in the land, God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; left him, to try him, that he might know all &#91;that was&#93; in his heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Now the rest of the acts of Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93;, and his goodness, behold, they &#91;are&#93; written in the vision of Yesha’yahu &#91;Yehovah has saved&#93; the prophet, the son of Amotz &#91;strong&#93;, &#91;and&#93; in the book of the kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; slept with his fathers, and they buried him in the most important of the sepulchres of the sons of David &#91;beloved&#93;: and all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; did him honour at his death. And M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; his son reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; &#91;was&#93; twelve years old when he began to reign, and he reigned fifty and five years in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> But did &#91;that which was&#93; evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), like unto the abominations of the heathen, whom the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had cast out before the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> For he built again the high places which Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93; his father had broken down, and he reared up altars for Ba’alim &#91;lords (false gods)&#93;, and made groves, and worshipped all the army of heaven, and served them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> Also he built altars in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), whereof the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had said, In Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; shall my name be forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And he built altars for all the army of heaven in the two courts of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> And he caused his children to pass through the fire in the valley of the son of Hinnom &#91;Lamentation&#93;: also he observed times, and used enchantments, and used witchcraft, and dealt with a familiar spirit, and with wizards: he wrought much evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to provoke him to anger.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> And he set a carved image, the idol which he had made, in the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, of which God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; had said to David &#91;beloved&#93; and to Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; his son, In this house, and in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, which I have chosen before all the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, will I put my name forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> Neither will I any more remove the foot of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; from out of the land which I have appointed for your fathers; so that they will take heed to do all that I have commanded them, according to the whole law and the statutes and the ordinances by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> So M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; made Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; to err, &#91;and&#93; to do worse than the heathen, whom the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had destroyed before the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke to M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, and to his people: but they would not hearken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> For what reason the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought upon them the captains of the host of the king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem &#91;name&#93; (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))), which took M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; among the thorns, and bound him with fetters, and carried him to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> And when he was in affliction, he besought the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and humbled himself greatly before the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of his fathers,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And prayed unto him: and he was intreated of him, and heard his supplication, and brought him again to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; into his kingdom. Then M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; knew that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) he &#91;was&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> Now after this he built a wall without the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;, on the west side of Gikhon (Bursting Forth), in the valley, even to the entering in at the fish gate, and compassed about ‘Ofel &#91;hill&#93;, and raised it up a very great height, and put captains of war in all the fenced cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And he took away the strange gods, and the idol out of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and all the altars that he had built in the mount of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and cast &#91;them&#93; out of the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> And he repaired the altar of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and sacrificed thereon peace offerings and thank offerings, and commanded Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; to serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> Nevertheless the people did sacrifice still in the high places, &#91;yet&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; only.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Now the rest of the acts of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, and his prayer unto his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and the words of the seers that spoke to him in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, behold, they &#91;are written&#93; in the book of the kings of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> His prayer also, and &#91;how God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;&#93; was intreated of him, and all his sin, and his trespass, and the places wherein he built high places, and set up groves and graven images, before he was humbled: behold, they &#91;are&#93; written among the sayings of the seers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> So M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; slept with his fathers, and they buried him in his own house: and ‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93; his son reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> ‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93; &#91;was&#93; two and twenty years old when he began to reign, and reigned two years in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> But he did &#91;that which was&#93; evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), as did M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; his father: for ‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93; sacrificed unto all the carved images which M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; his father had made, and served them;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And humbled not himself before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), as M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; his father had humbled himself; but ‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93; trespassed more and more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And his servants conspired against him, and killed him in his own house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> But the people of the land killed all them that had conspired against king ‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93;; and the people of the land made Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; his son king in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; &#91;was&#93; eight years old when he began to reign, and he reigned in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; one and thirty years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And he did &#91;that which was&#93; right in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and walked in the ways of David &#91;beloved&#93; his father, and declined &#91;neither&#93; to the right hand, nor to the left.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> For in the eighth year of his reign, while he was yet young, he began to seek after the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of David &#91;beloved&#93; his father: and in the twelfth year he began to purge Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; from the high places, and the groves, and the carved images, and the molten images.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> And they broke down the altars of Ba’alim &#91;lords (false gods)&#93; in his presence; and the images, that &#91;were&#93; on high above them, he cut down; and the groves, and the carved images, and the molten images, he broke in pieces, and made dust &#91;of them&#93;, and strewed &#91;it&#93; upon the graves of them that had sacrificed unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And he burnt the bones of the priests upon their altars, and cleansed Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> And &#91;so did he&#93; in the cities of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, and Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;, even unto Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;, with their mattocks round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> And when he had broken down the altars and the groves, and had beaten the graven images into powder, and cut down all the idols throughout all the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, he returned to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> Now in the eighteenth year of his reign, when he had purged the land, and the house, he sent Shafan &#91;coney&#93; the son of Atzalyahu &#91;Yehovah has set apart&#93;, and Ma’aseiyah &#91;work of Yehovah&#93; the governor of the city, and Yo’ach &#91;Yahovah is brother&#93; the son of Yo’achaz &#91;Yehovah has grasped&#93; the recorder, to repair the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> And when they came to Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93; the High Priest, they delivered the money that was brought into the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, which the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; that kept the doors had gathered of the hand of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; and Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and of all the remnant of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and of all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;; and they returned to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> And they put &#91;it&#93; in the hand of the workmen that had the oversight of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and they gave it to the workmen that wrought in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to repair and amend the house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> Even to the artificers and builders gave they &#91;it&#93;, to buy cut stone, and timber for couplings, and to floor the houses which the kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; had destroyed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> And the men did the work faithfully: and the overseers of them &#91;were&#93; Yachat &#91;He will snatch up&#93; and ‘Ovadyah &#91;servant of Yehovah&#93;, the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, of the sons of M’rari &#91;Bitter&#93;; and Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93; and Meshulam &#91;friend&#93;, of the sons of the K’hati, to set &#91;it&#93; forward; and &#91;other of&#93; the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, all that could skill of instruments of music.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> Also &#91;they were&#93; over the bearers of burdens, and &#91;were&#93; overseers of all that wrought the work in any manner of service: and of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;there were&#93; scribes, and officers, and porters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> And when they brought out the money that was brought into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93; the priest found a book of the law of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;given&#93; by Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93; answered and said to Shafan &#91;coney&#93; the scribe, I have found the book of the law in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). And Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93; delivered the book to Shafan &#91;coney&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> And Shafan &#91;coney&#93; carried the book to the king, and brought the king word back again, saying, All that was committed to youi servants, they do &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> And they have gathered together the money that was found in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and have delivered it into the hand of the overseers, and to the hand of the workmen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Then Shafan &#91;coney&#93; the scribe told the king, saying, Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93; the priest has given me a book. And Shafan &#91;coney&#93; read it before the king.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And it came to pass, when the king had heard the words of the law, that he rent his clothes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And the king commanded Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93;, and Achikam &#91;my brother has arisen&#93; the son of Shafan &#91;coney&#93;, and Avdon &#91;Servile&#93; the son of Mikhay’hu &#91;Who is Like God?&#93;, and Shafan &#91;coney&#93; the scribe, and ‘Asayah &#91;made by Yehovah&#93; a servant of the king’s, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Go, enquire of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) for me, and for them that are left in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, concerning the words of the book that is found: for great &#91;is&#93; the wrath of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that is poured out upon us, because our fathers have not kept the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to do after all that is written in this book.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93;, and &#91;they&#93; that the king &#91;had appointed&#93;, went to Huldah &#91;weasel&#93; the prophetess, the wife of Shalum &#91;retribution&#93; the son of Tikevah &#91;hope&#93;, the son of Hasrat &#91;lack&#93;, keeper of the wardrobe; (now she dwelt in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; in the college) and they spoke to her to that &#91;effect&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And she answered them, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Tell you2f the man that sent you to me,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Behold, I will bring evil upon this place, and upon the inhabitants thereof, &#91;even&#93; all the curses that are written in the book which they have read before the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Because they have forsaken me, and have burned incense unto other gods, that they might provoke me to anger with all the works of their hands; therefore my wrath shall be poured out upon this place, and shall not be quenched.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And as for the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, who sent you to enquire of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), so shall you2f say unto him, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;concerning&#93; the words which youi have heard;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Because youri heart was tender, and youi did humble yourself before God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, when youi heard his words against this place, and against the inhabitants thereof, and humbled yourself before me, and did rend (tear apart; forcefully rip apart) youri clothes, and weep before me; I have even heard &#91;youi&#93; also, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Behold, I will gather youi to youri fathers, and youi shall be gathered to youri grave in peace, neither shall youri eyes see all the evil that I will bring upon this place, and upon the inhabitants of the same. So they brought the king word again.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Then the king sent and gathered together all the elders of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And the king went up into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and all the men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and the priests, and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, and all the people, great and small: and he read in their ears all the words of the book of the covenant that was found in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And the king stood in his place, and made a covenant before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to walk after the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and to keep his commandments, and his testimonies, and his statutes, with all his heart, and with all his soul, to perform the words of the covenant which are written in this book.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And he caused all that were present in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; and Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93; to stand &#91;to it&#93;. And the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; did according to the covenant of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; took away all the abominations out of all the countries that &#91;pertained&#93; to the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and made all that were present in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to serve, &#91;even&#93; to serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;. &#91;And&#93; all his days they departed not from following the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Moreover Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; kept a Passover unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: and they killed the Passover on the fourteenth &#91;day&#93; of the first month.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> And he set the priests in their charges, and encouraged them to the service of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> And said unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; that taught all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which were holy unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Put &#91;a bow&#93; the holy ark in the house which Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; the son of David &#91;beloved&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; did build; &#91;it shall&#93; not &#91;be&#93; a burden upon &#91;your&#93; shoulders: serve now the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and his people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> And prepare &#91;yourselves&#93; by the houses of your fathers, after your courses, according to the writing of David &#91;beloved&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and according to the writing of Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; his son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> And stand in the holy &#91;place&#93; according to the divisions of the families of the fathers of your brethren the people, and &#91;after&#93; the division of the families of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> So kill the Passover, and sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) yourselves, and prepare your brethren, that &#91;they&#93; may do according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> And Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; gave to the people, of the flock, lambs and kids, all for the Passover offerings, for all that were present, to the number of thirty thousand, and three thousand bullocks: these &#91;were&#93; of the king’s substance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> And his princes gave willingly unto the people, to the priests, and to the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;: Hilkiyahu &#91;my portion is Yehovah&#93; and Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93; and Jehiel, rulers of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, gave unto the priests for the Passover offerings two thousand and six hundred &#91;small cattle&#93;, and three hundred oxen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> Konanyah &#91;Yehovah has established&#93; also, and Sh’may’yah &#91;Yehovah hears&#93; and N’tan’el &#91;God’s Gift&#93;, his brethren, and Hashavyah &#91;Yehovah has considered&#93; and Ye’i’el &#91;God sweeps away&#93; and Yozavad &#91;Yehovah has endowed&#93;, chief of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, gave unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; for Passover offerings five thousand &#91;small cattle&#93;, and five hundred oxen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> So the service was prepared, and the priests stood in their place, and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; in their courses, according to the king’s commandment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> And they killed the Passover, and the priests sprinkled &#91;the blood&#93; from their hands, and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; flayed &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> And they removed the burnt offerings, that they might give according to the divisions of the families of the people, to offer unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), as &#91;it is&#93; written in the book of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;. And so &#91;did they&#93; with the oxen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And they roasted the Passover with fire according to the ordinance: but the &#91;other&#93; holy &#91;offerings&#93; coocked (boiled) they in pots, and in caldrons, and in pans, and divided &#91;them&#93; speedily among all the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> And afterward they made ready for themselves, and for the priests: because the priests the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; &#91;were busied&#93; in offering of burnt offerings and the fat until night; therefore the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; prepared for themselves, and for the priests the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And the singers the sons of Asaf &#91;gatherer&#93; &#91;were&#93; in their place, according to the commandment of David &#91;beloved&#93;, and Asaf &#91;gatherer&#93;, and Heiman &#91;faithful&#93;, and Y’dutun &#91;praising&#93; the king’s seer; and the porters &#91;waited&#93; at every gate; they might not depart from their service; for their brethren the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; prepared for them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> So all the service of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was prepared the same day, to keep the Passover, and to offer burnt offerings upon the altar of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), according to the commandment of king Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that were present kept the Passover at that time, and the feast of unleavened bread seven days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> And there was no Passover like to that kept in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; from the days of Sh’mu’el &#91;His name is El&#93; the prophet; neither did all the kings of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; keep such a Passover as Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; kept, and the priests, and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, and all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that were present, and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> In the eighteenth year of the reign of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; was this Passover kept.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> After all this, when Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; had prepared the temple, N’kho &#91;lame&#93; king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; came up to fight against Kark’mish &#91;fortress of Chemosh (subdue)&#93; by Euphrates (to break forth, to gush): and Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; went out against him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> But he sent ambassadors to him, saying, What have I to do with youi, youi king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;? &#91;I come&#93; not against youi this day, but against the house which I have war: for God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; commanded me to make haste: refrain (restrain; refrein from acting) youi from &#91;meddling with&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, who &#91;is&#93; with me, that he destroy youi not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Nevertheless Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; would not turn his face from him, but disguised himself, that he might fight with him, and listened not unto the words of N’kho &#91;lame&#93; from the mouth of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and came to fight in the valley of Megiddo &#91;Place of Crowds&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And the archers shot at king Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93;; and the king said to his servants, Have me away; for I am sore wounded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> His servants therefore took him out of that chariot, and put him in the second chariot that he had; and they brought him to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and he died, and was buried in &#91;one of&#93; the sepulchres of his fathers. And all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; mourned for Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; lamented for Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93;: and all the singing men and the singing women spoke of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; in their lamentations to this day, and made them an ordinance in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and, behold, they &#91;are&#93; written in the lamentations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Now the rest of the acts of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93;, and his goodness, according to &#91;that which was&#93; written in the law of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And his deeds, first and last, behold, they &#91;are&#93; written in the book of the kings of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:25; 1 Kings 2:12; Genesis 39:2"> Then the people of the land took Y’ho’achaz &#91;Yehovah has seized&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93;, and made him king in his father’s stead in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:1; 1 Chronicles 27:1; 2 Chronicles 30:2"> Y’ho’achaz &#91;Yehovah has seized&#93; &#91;was&#93; twenty and three years old when he began to reign, and he reigned three months in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Chronicles 21:29; 1 Chronicles 16:39; Leviticus 1:1"> And the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; put him down at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and condemned the land in an hundred talents of silver and a talent (weight of money; weight; gift) of gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="2 Samuel 6:17; 1 Chronicles 15:25–28; 2 Samuel 6:2"> And the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; made Elyakim &#91;God sets up&#93; his brother king over Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and turned his name to Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93;. And N’kho &#91;lame&#93; took Y’ho’achaz &#91;Yehovah has seized&#93; his brother, and carried him to Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Exodus 31:2; Exodus 38:1–7; 1 Chronicles 2:19–20"> Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; &#91;was&#93; twenty and five years old when he began to reign, and he reigned eleven years in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: and he did &#91;that which was&#93; evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="1 Kings 3:4; 1 Kings 8:63; 1 Chronicles 29:21"> Against him came up N’vukhadnetzar &#91;May Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and bound him in fetters, to carry him to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 7:7–8; 1 John 5:14–15; John 16:23"> N’vukhadnetzar &#91;May Nebo protect the crown&#93; also carried of the vessels of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and put them in his temple at Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Chronicles 28:5; Isaiah 55:3; 2 Samuel 22:51–1"> Now the rest of the acts of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93;, and his abominations which he did, and that which was found in him, behold, they &#91;are&#93; written in the book of the kings of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;: and Y’hoyakhin &#91;Yehovah establishes&#93; his son reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:12–16; Genesis 13:16; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> Y’hoyakhin &#91;Yehovah establishes&#93; &#91;was&#93; eight years old when he began to reign, and he reigned three months and ten days in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: and he did &#91;that which was&#93; evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="1 Kings 3:9; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:7; Mishlei - Proverbs 2:2–6"> And when the year was expired, king N’vukhadnetzar &#91;May Nebo protect the crown&#93; sent, and brought him to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, with the goodly vessels of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and made Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; his brother king over Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Jacob 3:17; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:7; 1 Samuel 16:7"> Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; &#91;was&#93; one and twenty years old when he began to reign, and reigned eleven years in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Jacob 1:5; 2 Chronicles 9:22; Gosel 6:33"> And he did &#91;that which was&#93; evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;and&#93; humbled not himself before Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet &#91;speaking&#93; from the mouth of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="2 Chronicles 1:3; 1 Kings 4:24–25"> And he also rebelled against king N’vukhadnetzar &#91;May Nebo protect the crown&#93;, who had made him swear by God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: but he stiffened his neck, and hardened his heart from turning unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="1 Kings 4:26; 1 Kings 10:26–29; 2 Chronicles 9:25"> Moreover all the chief of the priests, and the people, transgressed very much after all the abominations of the heathen; and polluted the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) which he had consecrated in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:27; Isaiah 9:10; 1 Kings 10:27–29"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of their fathers sent to them by his messengers, rising up early, and sending; because he had compassion on his people, and on his dwelling place:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="2 Chronicles 9:28; 1 Kings 10:28–29"> But they mocked the messengers of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and despised his words, and misused his prophets, until the wrath of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) arose against his people, till &#91;there was&#93; no remedy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="2 Kings 10:29"> Therefore he brought upon them the king of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, who killed their young men with the sword in the house of their sanctuary, and had no compassion upon young man or virgin, old man, or him that stooped for age: he gave &#91;them&#93; all into his hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> And all the vessels of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, great and small, and the treasures of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and the treasures of the king, and of his princes; all &#91;these&#93; he brought to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And they burnt the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and broke down the wall of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and burnt all the palaces thereof with fire, and destroyed all the goodly vessels thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And them that had escaped from the sword carried he away to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;; where they were servants to him and his sons until the reign of the kingdom of Paras (Persia) &#91;pure or splendid&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> To fulfil the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) by the mouth of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, until the land had enjoyed her Shabbat: &#91;for&#93; as long as she lay desolate she kept Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93;, to fulfil threescore and ten years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Now in the first year of Koresh (Cyrus) &#91;possess you the furnace&#93; king of Paras (Persia) &#91;pure or splendid&#93;, that the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;spoken&#93; by the mouth of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; might be accomplished, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) stirred up the spirit of Koresh (Cyrus) &#91;possess you the furnace&#93; king of Paras (Persia) &#91;pure or splendid&#93;, that he made a proclamation throughout all his kingdom, and &#91;put it&#93; also in writing, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Thus says Koresh (Cyrus) &#91;possess you the furnace&#93; king of Paras (Persia) &#91;pure or splendid&#93;, All the kingdoms of the earth has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of heaven given me; and he has charged me to build him an house in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;. Who &#91;is there&#93; among you of all his people? The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;be&#93; with him, and let him go up.</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
